# Painter engine prototype — source-code reuse (2026-09-16)

`scripts/painter/vendor/painter_flood_fill.gd` adapts the MIT-licensed
`src/Classes/FloodFillObject.gd` from Orama Interactive's Pixelorama, pinned to
commit `da7b68f97806c461abde615d1d847af91921c37b`:
https://github.com/Orama-Interactive/Pixelorama/tree/da7b68f97806c461abde615d1d847af91921c37b.
Copyright (c) 2019-present Orama Interactive and contributors. The full MIT
notice is retained in `scripts/painter/vendor/LICENSE.txt`; upstream source and
LF-normalized hashes are in `FloodFillObject.upstream.txt` and `provenance.json`.
The original Shawn Hargreaves/Allegro algorithm attribution is retained.
Modifications: rename class, remove editor Project/selection coupling, guard
seed bounds, use exact RGBA comparison. No Pixelorama art, fonts or audio are
imported. Existing Roshan graphics and recorded voice are referenced unchanged
under their existing provenance; only a separate child-authored canvas changes.

## Door highlight rewrite review composites — 2026-09-30

Diagnostic evidence for `CODEX_DOOR_HIGHLIGHT_REWRITE_HANDOFF_2026-09-30.md`.
Every image is a deterministic Pillow composite of approved project art (the
Main Hall master tiles, hall door signs, Moonflower dormant and available
cards and Sky Lagoon mist wisp) at the 1280×720 stage scale. It inherits each depicted source
asset's existing license and private-use restrictions and grants no new rights.
No source art is modified. The images are review-only, excluded from runtime by
`audit/.gdignore`, and are not runtime capture evidence.

- `audit/door_highlight_rewrite_2026-09-30/current_day_one_bubble_bath_entry.jpg` — replica of the `scripts/castle_door_cue.gd` drawing at dev `e7899cc0`, Day One beat 1, dev spawn camera; generated by `render_review_mocks.py`.
- `audit/door_highlight_rewrite_2026-09-30/concept_day_one_bubble_bath_entry.jpg` — proposed Door Guidance v2 concept for the same frame; generated by `render_review_mocks.py`.
- `audit/door_highlight_rewrite_2026-09-30/squint_quarter_scale_current_vs_concept.jpg` — quarter-scale squint pair of the two frames above; generated by `render_review_mocks.py`.
- `audit/door_highlight_rewrite_2026-09-30/current_day_one_wandered_left.jpg` — current-cue replica, Mermaid Pool beat, camera at the hall's left end; generated by `render_review_mocks.py`.
- `audit/door_highlight_rewrite_2026-09-30/concept_day_one_wandered_left.jpg` — concept with the edge beacon for the same frame; generated by `render_review_mocks.py`.
- `audit/door_highlight_rewrite_2026-09-30/current_free_play_royal_event_entry.jpg` — free-play hall entry with a Royal Hall event off-screen, current runtime look; generated by `render_review_mocks.py`.
- `audit/door_highlight_rewrite_2026-09-30/concept_free_play_royal_event_entry.jpg` — concept edge beacon for the same frame; generated by `render_review_mocks.py`.
- `audit/door_highlight_rewrite_2026-09-30/measured_door_geometry_overlay.jpg` — master-tile crops with measured opening (green), frame edge (magenta) and current hotspot rects (yellow); generated by `measure_hall_door_geometry.py`, which also writes `measured_door_geometry.json` with source tile SHA-256 values.
- `audit/door_highlight_rewrite_2026-09-30/measured_moonflower_gate_overlay.jpg` — the approved Moonflower butterfly gate card with today's touch box (yellow), measured doorway frame edges (magenta) and opening edges, apex and threshold (green); generated by `measure_hall_door_geometry.py`.
